The base tier leverages an automated question-and-answer mechanism to generate basic visual attribute descriptions; the intermediate tier enhances fine-grained detail using an inter-sample contrastive mechanism; the advanced tier further enriches textual diversity via a stylized expansion mechanism. In the retrieval stage, to mitigate the impact of noisy pseudo texts, we develop an adaptive confidence-weighted retrieval learning framework. We model image-text pairs as clean or noisy using a Gaussian Mixture Model, calibrated by real-time image-text similarity and static text generation probability from the prior stage, yielding adaptive sample weights during training. Beyond that, we also contribute LargeFine-Person, a large-scale TBPS dataset with high-quality, fine-grained, and diverse textual annotations, enabling a practical and generalizable TBPS pre-training benchmark under unsupervised setting. Experiments on multiple TBPS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of both GTR+ and LargeFine-Person. Code is available at: https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/GTR.

</details>


### [68] [Investigating Temporal Motion Features for Pose-to-Text Indian Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12993)
*Manav Dhamecha,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究T5规模与显式运动特征对手语翻译的影响，发现T5-small加运动特征效果最佳，系统在WSLP 2026排名第5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练T5模型规模及显式运动特征对姿态到手语翻译（印度手语）性能的影响，参与WSLP 2026共享任务。

Method: 通过轻量级姿态编码器将姿态序列投影到T5嵌入空间，微调整个模型生成英文文本；对比T5-small、T5-base、T5-large，并提出加入逐帧姿态差分的运动增强变体T5-small + Motion。

Result: 测试集5334例、验证集5257例。纯空间模型中T5-small的BLEU和ROUGE最佳，T5-large的chrF最高；T5-small + Motion带来最大单次提升，BLEU显著超过空间基线，成为该指标最强模型。系统官方榜单排名第5。

Conclusion: 显式运动特征比单纯扩大模型规模更能提升姿态到手语翻译性能；代码与模型已在GitHub和HuggingFace公开。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of pretrained T5 model scale and explicit motion features on pose-to-text Indian Sign Language Translation (SLT) for the WSLP 2026 Shared Task. Pose sequences are projected into the embedding space of T5 through a lightweight pose encoder, with the complete model fine-tuned to generate English text. The shared task data used for this work consists of a test set with 5,334 examples and a validation set with 5,257 examples. We compare T5-small, T5-base, and T5-large, and additionally introduce a motion-augmented variant, T5-small + Motion, that adds explicit frame-to-frame pose differences to the input representation. T5-small achieves the best BLEU and ROUGE scores among the spatial-only models, while T5-large obtains the highest chrF score. Augmenting T5-small with motion features yields the largest single improvement observed in our study, substantially improving BLEU over the spatial-only baseline and making it the strongest model overall on this metric. Our submitted system ranked 5th on the official WSLP 2026 SLT testing leaderboard. The source code and trained models are publicly available on GitHub and HuggingFace.

</details>


### [69] [SV-Cine: Diagnosis-Conditioned Segmentation of Single Ventricle Physiology via Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12997)
*Lila Cunge,Yuehong Liu,Hang Xu,Thomas Coudert,Pierangelo Renella,J Paul Finn,William Hsu,Kim-Lien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向单心室生理（SVP）的心脏MRI分割框架，结合合成数据增强与诊断条件化的基础模型适配（SV-Cine），显著提升右心室分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVP是罕见的先天性心脏病亚型，仅有单一功能性心室且解剖结构异常，临床数据稀缺、形态多样，导致传统图像分割方法难以应用，亟需针对性的稳健分割方案。

Method: 首先构建数据增强流程，利用SDF4CHD生成合成3D心脏网格，并通过生成建模得到对应的合成心脏MRI；其次提出SV-Cine，在基础模型CineMA上引入基于患者诊断信息的Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）层，实现诊断感知的特征自适应分割。

Result: 在包含多种SVP亚型的内部队列评估中，SV-Cine左心室中位Dice为0.89（IQR 0.80–0.91），右心室为0.72（IQR 0.54–0.84），右心室分割比最强基线nnU-Net高出0.39 Dice点；主心室中位射血分数误差为5.55个百分点（IQR 3.41–7.69）。外部队列的左心室和心肌分割性能低于内部队列，而右心室Dice在两队列间相当。

Conclusion: 预训练基础模型可通过引入诊断先验适配到高度专门化的下游任务，同时利用大规模MRI预训练中获得的解剖知识，从而在数据稀缺的SVP场景下取得良好分割效果。

Abstract: Single Ventricle Physiology (SVP) is a rare subtype of congenital heart disease characterized by the presence of a single functional cardiac ventricle with atypical anatomic configurations that challenge conventional image segmentation approaches. The scarcity of clinical data and the morphological diversity across SVP subtypes make the development of robust segmentation methods particularly difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a cardiac MRI segmentation framework focused on ventricular chambers and myocardium segmentation tailored for SVP. First, we introduce a data augmentation pipeline that generates synthetic 3D cardiac meshes using SDF4CHD and corresponding synthetic cardiac MRI through generative modeling. Second, we introduce SV-Cine, a diagnosis-conditioned adaptation of the foundation model CineMA that incorporates patient-level diagnostic information through Feature-wise Linear Modulation layers, enabling diagnosis-aware feature adaptation during segmentation. We evaluated the framework on an internal cohort with varying SVP subtypes. SV-Cine achieved median Dice scores of 0.89 (IQR: 0.80--0.91) for the left ventricle and 0.72 (IQR: 0.54--0.84) for the right ventricle, outperforming the strongest baseline, nnU-Net, by 0.39 Dice points on right ventricle segmentation. It also yields a median ejection fraction error of 5.55 percentage points (IQR: 3.41--7.69) for the dominant ventricle. Compared with the internal cohort, LV and myocardium segmentation performance was lower for the external cohort; whereas RV Dice scores were comparable for both cohorts. Our findings suggest that a pretrained foundation model can be adapted for highly specialized downstream tasks through usage of diagnosis priors while leveraging anatomic knowledge learned from large-scale MRI datasets during pretraining.

</details>


### [70] [Physics-Aware Video Generation via Agentic Planning and Graph-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13006)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysPlan，一种无需训练的视频扩散引导框架，用VLM作为认知模拟器并以对象为中心的测试时优化，提升生成视频的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型缺乏对物理规律的内在理解，常生成视觉美观但因果不合理（结构幻觉、物理不可行动态）的视频。通过训练-free测试时优化注入物理意识是可行方向，但现有方法依赖全局梯度更新和刚性调度启发式，会破坏被动背景且无法建模复杂动态状态变化。

Method: PhysPlan无需训练。首先由VLM作为迭代认知模拟器，将多模态输入分解为视觉思维链，构建包含运动学轨迹与3D深度几何的多模态表征；其次，这些信号驱动对象中心式测试时优化。方法包括对象中心梯度路由，以隔离运动学修改并完全锁定被动环境；以及运动强度刻画，动态参数化超参数以适配不同程度的物理形变。

Result: 在PhyGenBench和Physics-IQ基准上的大量评估表明，PhysPlan显著优于基础VDM和可控VDM基线，为提高视频生成的物理理解提供了有前景的途径。

Conclusion: PhysPlan将范式从随机视觉插值转向智能体物理模拟，通过对象中心梯度路由与动力学强度刻画，在无需训练的情况下有效改善视频扩散模型的物理合理性。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity, photorealistic video content. However, they fundamentally lack an intrinsic understanding of physical laws and frequently produce visually appealing but causally illogical sequences characterized by structural hallucinations and physically implausible dynamics. Injecting physical awareness via training-free test-time optimization is a promising alternative, yet existing methods rely on global gradient updates and rigid scheduling heuristics that inadvertently corrupt passive backgrounds and fail to model complex dynamic state changes. To address this, we propose PhysPlan, a novel training-free guidance framework that shifts the paradigm from stochastic visual interpolation to agentic physics simulation. First, a VLM operates as an iterative cognitive simulator, decomposing multimodal inputs into a Chain-of-Visual-Thought to create a multimodal representation of kinematic trajectories and 3D depth geometries. Second, these signals drives an object-centric test-time optimization. Unlike prior training-free methods that rely on global gradients and rigid scheduling heuristics, PhysPlan introduces Object-Centric Gradient Routing to isolate kinematic modifications and completely lock the passive environment. Furthermore, our Kinetic Intensity Profiling dynamically parameterizes framework hyperparameters to accommodate the varying severity of physical deformations. Extensive evaluations on the PhyGenBench and Physics-IQ benchmarks demonstrate that PhysPlan significantly outperforms both foundational and controllable VDM baselines, offering a promising approach for improving the physical understanding of video generation.

</details>


### [71] [Pixel Decodability Is Not a Compression Signal: Causally Evaluating Importance Proxies for Visual KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13012)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型的视觉KV缓存中保留了大量可像素解码的视觉内容，但这种保留是"任务惰性"的：保留量与计算是否因果依赖该单元无关，且作为KV压缩信号不如随机或注意力幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉KV缓存中"可像素解码的保留量"是否能反映该单元对回答任务的因果重要性，从而判断它能否作为KV缓存压缩（驱逐）的有效信号。

Method: 用学习到的像素逆变换解码器测量保留量；用单超级块KV消融、教师强制下金标准答案对数概率下降来测量因果使用；在预注册、符号校准、留出的实验设计下，在图像内关联两者，并对比注意力代理，考察不同架构、不同粒度下的表现。

Result: 保留量与注意力解耦，且在统计功效充足的零结果中与因果利用无关；注意力虽弱但显著地追踪因果利用，是唯一具有该信号的指标（作为阳性对照）。架构间差异明显：无编码器模型比有编码器模型多保留2.7倍。超级块粒度下，去混淆的可像素解码保留量作为KV驱逐排序不比随机更好；令牌粒度下仅在较大预算时获得微弱的逆重要性信号，且在每个预算下都被注意力幅度支配。

Conclusion: 可像素解码的重建性构成视觉KV缓存的一个信息轴，与功能轴正交；在所测的任何粒度下，它都不是有竞争力的KV压缩信号，仅是一个受控的负面结果。

Abstract: Vision-language models retain a substantial amount of pixel-decodable visual content in their visual key-value cache. We show, in our setting, that this retention is task-inert: across our preregistered tests, how much a unit retains never positively tracks whether the computation that answers the question causally relies on it. We measure retention with a learned pixel-inversion decoder and causal use with single-super-patch KV ablation, the teacher-forced drop in gold-answer log-probability, and relate the two within images under a preregistered, sign-calibrated, held-out design. Retention is decoupled from attention and, in a well-powered null, from causal utilization. Utilization is not inert to every proxy: attention weakly but significantly tracks it, the only signal we find that does and the design's positive control. We characterize pixel-decodable retention as an informational axis of the visual KV cache, orthogonal to the functional one. How much task-inert content a cache holds differs by architecture in our model pair: the encoder-free model retains 2.7 times more than the encoder-based one. The engineering consequence is a controlled negative result. At super-patch granularity, deconfounded pixel-decodable retention ranks KV eviction no better than random; at token granularity it acquires only a weak inverse-importance signal at larger budgets, dominated at every budget by attention magnitude. In our setting, pixel-decodable reconstructability is not a competitive KV-compression signal at any granularity we test.

</details>


### [72] [TileNet: Tile-Based CNN-SVM Architecture for Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Systems Inspection of Flat Roofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13013)
*Samuel Dunthorne,Hashim A. Hashim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无人机(UAS)的实时深度学习框架，通过双高度航拍与轻量级CNN-SVM分类器，在嵌入式硬件上实现屋顶缺陷的自主检测，测试准确率达94.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平屋顶是建筑围护结构中最关键的部件之一，直接影响结构性能与热效率，进而关系到家庭能耗、碳排放及长期环境可持续性。及时检测屋顶缺陷对于减少冷暖损失、防止霉菌等湿气引起的退化、支撑国家气候减缓目标至关重要。然而嵌入式无人机硬件的算力与功耗受限，需要兼顾精度与实时性的检测方案。

Method: 提出实时UAS深度学习框架，利用双高度飞行中捕获的实时影像自主检测缺陷。采用多分辨率飞行策略以同时识别细小缺陷和较大结构问题。为满足嵌入式硬件的计算与功耗约束，框架结合了基于图块(tile)的架构与轻量级CNN-SVM分类器，用于低延迟机载推理。最终模型包含5个卷积层和4个全连接层，最后一层为线性SVM头。数据集为图块化与增强图像，按照片级划分：训练43,383张、验证3,869张、测试2,540张。

Result: 模型在三组随机种子下取得平均测试准确率94.4%（95%置信区间±0.4%），优于GoogLeNet（89.2%）和AlexNet（79.8%）。使用DJI Matrice 350 RTK无人机现场采集的真实UAS影像进行实验评估，系统支持快速、可重复且安全的屋顶巡检。

Conclusion: 该系统可降低人工风险、减少运营成本，并促进更可持续的建筑维护，展示了轻量级机载深度学习在屋顶缺陷实时检测中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Flat roofs are among the most influential components of the building envelope, governing both structural performance and thermal efficiency, and thereby contributing directly to household energy consumption, carbon emissions, and long-term environmental sustainability. Timely detection of roof defects is essential for reducing heating and cooling losses, preventing moisture-driven degradation such as mold growth, and supporting national climate-change mitigation goals. This paper presents a real-time, Unmanned Aerial System (UAS)-based deep learning framework that autonomously detects defects using live imagery captured during dual-altitude aerial passes. The multi-resolution flight strategy is designed to aid the identification of both small, fine-scale defects and larger structural issues, enabling more comprehensive assessments. To meet the strict computational and power constraints of embedded UAS hardware, the proposed framework integrates a tile-based architecture with a lightweight Convolution Neural Network-Support Vector Machine (CNN-SVM) classifier designed for low-latency onboard inference. The final model-comprising five convolutional layers and four dense layers, the last a linear SVM head, achieved a mean test accuracy of $94.4\%$ ($95\%$ confidence interval $\pm0.4\%$ over three seeds) on a photo-level split ($43,383$ training, $3,869$ validation, and $2,540$ test tiled and augmented images), outperforming GoogLeNet ($89.2\%$) and AlexNet ($79.8\%$). Experimental evaluations using real UAS imagery collected by onsite visits with DJI Matrice 350 RTK drone demonstrate that the system supports rapid, repeatable, and safe roof inspections while reducing human risk, lowering operational costs, and enabling more sustainable building maintenance.

</details>


### [73] [Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation for 3D-CNNs in Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13024)
*Yanjiang Shi,Peng Zhao,Nan Qi,Guiqin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出标签引导知识蒸馏（LGKD），通过样本级和类别级蒸馏，指导3D-CNN学生模型学习教师特征，在UCF101与HMDB51上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分析中的特征蒸馏方法多为图像分析方法的简单迁移，忽视了视频特征在时间维度上的差异。

Method: LGKD包含两部分：样本级蒸馏利用标签信息与教师概率分布，指导对时间精度影响显著的特征学习并抑制噪声；类别级蒸馏借助原型网络捕捉同类样本间的关系知识，提升高维语义学习与泛化能力。

Result: 在UCF101和HMDB51两个动作识别基准数据集上进行了充分实验，取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: LGKD能有效利用标签引导特征蒸馏，弥补视频特征时间维度上的不足，提升3D-CNN学生模型性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: As a key model compression technique, knowledge distillation aims to transfer knowledge from a high-capacity teacher model to a lightweight student model for enhancing the latter's performance. In this work, we reviewed the feature knowledge distillation for 3D-CNNs and observed that most feature distillation methods in video analysis are simple adaptations of those used in image analysis, often neglecting the differences of video features in the temporal dimension. To address this issue, we proposed Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation (LGKD) to guide the distillation of student model features using ground truth labels. Our method entails two components: sample-wise distillation and class-wise distillation, enabling the student model to learn feature representation of the teacher model at two levels. Sample-wise distillation utilizes label information and the teacher's probability distribution to guide the learning of features that significantly impact temporal accuracy while mitigating noise. Meanwhile, class-wise feature distillation employs a prototype network to further capture the relational knowledge among samples within the same category, enhancing the student's ability to learn higher-dimensional semantic information and improving model generalization. To demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method, we conducted comprehensive experiments on two benchmark action recognition datasets, UCF101 and HMDB51, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [74] [Unified CT and MRI Pancreas Segmentation for Label-Efficient Cross-Modality Subregion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13043)
*Ziliang Hong,Hongyi Pan,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Bejar,Elif Keles,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一3D胰腺分割框架，用域对抗学习在4604例CT/MRI上学习解剖表征，实现跨模态全胰腺分割与标签高效的下游子区迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像跨模态分割困难：不同成像模态在外观和强度分布上差异大，单模态训练的模型迁移到未见域时性能显著下降。目标是学习统一的解剖表征以支持跨模态分割。

Method: 采用域对抗学习，在4604例异质CT和MRI扫描上训练共享的nnU-Net编码器-解码器进行全胰腺分割，并引入隐空间域判别器促进CT-MRI特征对齐；随后将学到的编码器迁移到胰腺头/体/尾子区分割，仅使用有限的MRI子区标注。

Result: 全胰腺分割：分布内测试集平均Dice 87.31%，外部OOD数据集Dice在84.20%–88.09%之间。下游子区分割：MRI Dice 80.53%，CT Dice 83.05%，且未使用CT子区标注。

Conclusion: 统一的解剖表征可同时支持跨模态胰腺分割与标签高效的下游任务迁移。

Abstract: Robust medical image segmentation across imaging modalities is challenging because of large differences in appearance and intensity distributions. Models trained on a single modality often show substantial performance drops when applied to unseen domains. In this work, we develop a unified 3D pancreas segmentation framework that applies domain-adversarial learning to 4,604 heterogeneous CT and MRI scans to learn anatomical representations. A shared nnU-Net encoder-decoder is trained for whole-pancreas segmentation, with a latent domain discriminator encouraging CT-MRI feature alignment. The learned encoder is subsequently transferred to pancreatic head-body-tail segmentation using limited MRI-only subregion annotations. An average Dice score of 87.31% on the in-distribution test set and Dice scores ranging from 84.20% to 88.09% across external OOD datasets were achieved in whole pancreas segmentation. Dice scores of 80.53% on MRI and 83.05% on CT were achieved for downstream subregion segmentation, without using CT subregion annotations. These results demonstrate that a unified anatomical representation can support both cross-modality pancreas segmentation and label-efficient downstream transfer.

</details>

### [75] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Occamy-1.0，一个通过后训练 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 得到的高性价比协作智能体模型，在协作工作基准上达到同等规模最强水平，并处于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作智能体需在多次模型调用中完成信息收集、工具使用、编码与文件操作等复杂工作流，成本与延迟在整个任务周期内累积。其实际价值不仅取决于峰值能力，也取决于能力交付的效率。而日常工作中的许多步骤更强调状态跟踪、协调、恢复与执行到底，而非前沿规模推理，因此需要成本高效的专用模型。

Method: 对已后训练的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 检查点进行进一步训练。具体包括：构建基于执行的数据与环境；在多种 harness 下采集可重放的长时程轨迹；采用分阶段后训练来逐步培养并固化互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛的协作工作基准套件上，Occamy-1.0 持续位居同等规模模型中最强之列，并在若干任务上可与规模远大的前沿系统竞争。在既定评测与定价协议下，其在四个代表性基准上的综合表现处于观测到的成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。工具调用、编码与指令遵循等支撑性评测进一步表明该专业化保留了广泛的智能体能力。

Conclusion: Occamy-1.0 是一种成本高效的协作智能体模型，证明了面向执行能力的分阶段后训练可在控制成本的同时保持广泛智能体能力。作者发布了模型权重及部分训练数据，以支持实用协作智能体与智能体后训练的研究。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [76] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LAMAE，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段通过共享潜在注意力模块在潜空间直接交换信息，实现患者级表示学习，在多项多模态住院任务上超越模态特定预训练及强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声心动图、胸片和临床变量等互补模态的整合，每个模态捕捉心脏生理不同但相关的方面。然而现有多数医学基础模型仍是模态特定的，仅在微调或后训练阶段组合模态，这丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据，也忽略了每个模态内部的结构。

Method: LAMAE是一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练期间联合学习患者级表示。它不在事后融合模态，而是通过一个共享的潜在注意力模块，在study-view-entity层级结构上直接在潜空间中交换信息，从而实现变量观测的聚合以及对缺失模态的优雅处理。在超过120万MIMIC-IV住院记录上预训练。

Result: 在多模态住院任务（如院内死亡率、ICD-10和DRG编码、住院时长）上，LAMAE优于模态特定预训练以及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，同时在单模态任务上保持竞争力。即使测试时仅有一个模态可用，这些增益仍然存在。

Conclusion: 对模态内和模态间结构同时建模可产生更稳健、更可迁移的表示，LAMAE验证了在潜空间进行结构感知的多模态自监督预训练的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [77] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证语言描述是对定量记录的有损编码，损失不可逆，因此LLM无法胜任高风险定量决策；作者据此提出"大型定量模型（LQM）"这一独立模型类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设：定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、网络入侵阻断等重大定量决策的进步将源自大语言模型（LLM）的进步。作者认为这一假设存在根本缺陷，需要澄清。

Method: 将问题形式化为模型所训练表示（representation）的属性，而非模型容量（capacity）的属性；并识别高风险场景对模型提出的四项要求：可复现性、从每个输出回溯至源记录的血缘（lineage）、以及校准的不确定性，指出语言基底在构造上无法满足这些要求。

Result: 论证表明：描述是对定量记录的有损编码，损失不可逆——任何下游模型无论规模多大，都无法从描述中恢复描述本身未编码的信息；因此单靠扩大LLM规模无法弥合这一差距。

Conclusion: 上述属性共同界定了一个独立的模型类别，作者称之为大型定量模型（Large Quantitative Model, LQM），区别于以语言为基底的LLM。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [78] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DU-NO，一种多尺度U形谱神经算子，仅在编码器和解码器最浅的两个层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支，以3.64M参数实现近岸波浪预报中优于U-FNO的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解析波浪模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）精度高但成本昂贵，难以支撑集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警等业务化需求。现有高精度神经算子（如U-FNO）虽有求解器级精度，但参数量达数千万，规模过大。

Method: 提出DU-NO（Double U-shaped Neural Operator），一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在最浅的两层编码器和解码器处附加轻量卷积U-Net分支。其设计基于采样论证：高波数内容只存在于精细网格上，因此局部全频带通路置于高波数所在位置，而粗糙的带限层级保持纯谱结构；并采用随深度衰减的模态调度，将参数量控制在3.64M。

Result: 在公开的FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO在六个同构训练架构中取得最佳自回归滚动误差，相比U-FNO误差降低14.9%且参数少10.8倍；频带分析显示增益覆盖所有频带，包括截断谱算子失效的高波数带。参数匹配对照表明增益源于架构本身：在3.6M预算下最佳基线仍落后DU-NO 28.6%。该优势还延伸到2D Navier-Stokes（与最强基线持平）及PDEBench浅水滚动（明显胜出）。

Conclusion: DU-NO通过将卷积分支精准放置于高波数内容所在的浅层，以远小于U-FNO的参数量在波浪主导场及更广泛PDE任务上取得更优或相当的精度，验证了架构设计（而非单纯参数规模）带来的增益。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [79] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，用于规范ALD和ALE的实验与模拟过程数据，以解决文献数据异构、难以比较和机器复用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD和ALE在材料科学的实验与模拟文献中报道方式异构，导致数据难以比较和机器可操作复用。

Method: 基于schema-miner整理，并借助schema-miner pro以QUDT为本体基础，构建四个JSON Schema，涵盖材料、工艺条件、配置及测量/预测结果；并比较其范围、结构与语义基础。

Result: 展示了这些Schema在模式引导的文献抽取以及通过ORKG模板发布结构化记录中的应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的JSON Schema有助于实现ALD和ALE过程数据的标准化、可比较与机器可复用。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [80] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究draft-verify-revise流水线中的"指示语转换"（deictic shift）现象：同一上下文依赖表达式（如"previous"）在不同阶段被LLM解析为不同指称对象，进而影响meta-evaluator判定正确读法的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: draft-verify-revise是扩展推理时计算的常见LLM编排模式，但上下文在阶段间级联传递时，不同阶段的模型可能对"previous"等上下文依赖表达式产生不同解析，发生指示语转换，从而威胁流水线的可靠性。作者希望系统刻画该现象及其对最终判定准确率的影响。

Method: 构建合成数据集：10个基础样例，每个渲染为三种条件，保持共享成分不变，仅改变是draft阶段（assistant）还是verify阶段（grader）正确解析表达式，以及revise阶段（meta-evaluator）需要多少独立推理才能判断正确读法。测试3家提供商的6个模型、21种推理强度配置，采用e-values进行序贯检验；设主实验与消融实验（移除grader反馈中的错误分类标签）。另用独立LLM分析meta-evaluator给出错误判定时陈述的理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权均值）跨度极大，从低于随机水平的0.156到接近完美。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156升至最高推理强度下的0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有强度下均保持0.94以上，且其低推理强度即超过GPT-5.2的xhigh水平，每次试验成本约仅为其5%。meta-evaluator出错时倾向依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 指示语转换会显著影响draft-verify-revise流水线的判定可靠性，且该影响高度依赖模型的推理强度与推理能力配置。实施此类流水线的上下文工程师应警惕指示语转换，并在每个阶段显式指明预期指称对象。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [81] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB及对抗模仿学习方法GLARE，用于生成并评估多轮会议延续内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续需在长时多方可对话中追踪议程、发言角色、参与者意图与分歧，现有方法缺乏面向未来多轮延续的评估基准与针对性学习框架。

Method: 构建MDFB基准，基于2207场真实会议、24794条面向未来的查询；模型在给定转写前缀与当前问题下一次性生成多轮延续。评估两个维度：效用（向问题推进）与拟人性（对话流畅与角色一致性），不要求精确复现真实未来。提出GLARE，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成：判别器将观测延续排在当前actor样本之上，其分数作为KL正则化策略奖励，并通过在当前策略负样本上重训练使奖励景观随actor演化。

Result: GLARE在人工评估中效用平均胜率0.66、拟人性0.70，优于SFT与SPIN，但仍低于真实人类延续。MDFB还可作为社会推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断比较通用模型（含闭源系统）。

Conclusion: 该基准同时支持任务特定学习与基于输出的会议行为评估。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [82] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化流水线，生成模拟真实企业职场场景的合成邮件数据集，包含长/短形式问题及有据可依的参考答案，用于评估企业级深度研究智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中的问答智能体（依赖RAG、Deep Research等技术）面临信息分散于不断演变且可能相互冲突的邮件、聊天、文档等复杂数据中的挑战。现有基准缺乏真实世界复杂性，多为短文本回答和不自然查询，无法捕捉企业场景难点，因此亟需更贴近真实、高复杂度的评测数据。

Method: 作者构建了一个自动化流水线来生成合成邮件数据集，模拟跨越数月、涉及多达25名多角色交互员工的长周期企业项目。数据强调歧义性、信息分散性和自然产生的查询，并配套生成长短形式问题与基于数据的有据答案。

Result: 作者使用最新前沿模型，在数据集上评估了几个标准智能体基线。结果显示，各数据集在所有查询上的平均聚合得分均低于80%，表明仍有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: 结果表明企业部署仍有大量工作需完成，并凸显了构建真实、高复杂度评测数据对于开发更强真实世界企业DR系统的重要性。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [83] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Soft-PNet 将概念 grounding 重新表述为在预计算可行性符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 游走，用单锚点原型分布引导，并以统一的 KL 目标训练，从而去掉任务特定的可微损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型通常只在最终标签上监督，中间概念未被观测；同一标签可对应多种概念赋值，导致模型标签正确但概念错误，即推理捷径。原型网络通过将概念锚定到少量标注样本来缓解该问题，但现有方法仍依赖手工设计、任务特定的可微损失，每换一个任务就要重新设计。

Method: 提出 Soft-PNet：移除上述手工损失，把概念 grounding 看作在预计算的可行性符号解缓存上进行 Metropolis 游走，由每个概念一个标注锚点构建的原型分布来引导；训练时只用一个 KL 目标，对齐原型加权的缓存分布与网络的概念预测。该目标跨任务一致，且在解空间无法枚举时仍适用。

Result: 在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 上、稀缺监督条件下，Soft-PNet 在概念级和标签级上匹配了经过损失工程的原型网络，并恢复了软 grounding 基线遗漏的概念，同时无需损失工程且训练时间更低。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 通过将概念 grounding 转化为缓存上的 Metropolis 游走并采用统一 KL 目标，消除了任务特定的可微损失设计，在多个推理任务上保持与损失工程原型网络相当的性能，并具有更好的概念恢复能力和更低训练开销。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [84] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GT Bench 基准与 Graph Theory Agent（GTA），系统评估大语言模型在多种图表示下的图算法推理能力，并通过表示选择与分解式规划显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理评测存在局限：任务简单、图规模小、只评测代码生成而非图上推理本身，或固定单一输入格式，导致 LLM 进行多步图算法的可靠性不明确。

Method: 构建 GT Bench，涵盖 24 类经典图问题、44 种任务-结构设置、超 10 万样例，覆盖自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表、邻接矩阵四种表示；评估八个 LLM。进而提出 GTA，将偏好训练的表示选择器与计划-分解脚手架组合，围绕冻结的执行 LLM 工作。

Result: 准确率与输入表示强相关，最佳表示随图密度、规模、拓扑及模型变化；该敏感性在最强推理模型中减弱但仍存在。GTA 将 Phi-4 在 easy split 上从 53.5% 提升到 69.1%，hard split 上从 33.0% 提升到 41.5%，优于八个提示与智能体基线，并可无重训练迁移到 GraCoRe 和 NLGraph。

Conclusion: 图算法推理性能高度依赖表示选择与任务分解；通过表示选择与计划-分解智能体可有效提升 LLM 图推理能力并具备跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [85] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架，用Oracle Transformer模型模拟用户回答，与FastCA约束获取引擎交互，从而在无需领域知识的情况下自动学习出可用、一致且可解释的约束网络，显著减少人工交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束获取（CA）方法依赖与人类oracle的大量交互，学习过程在时间和查询次数上代价高昂。作者希望减少用户参与，实现自动化的约束获取。

Method: 提出神经符号框架：引入神经Oracle Transformer模型，基于已有样例训练，学习模拟用户回答并泛化概念知识；该学习到的oracle与专门的CA引擎FastCA交互，FastCA系统性地将oracle的回答精炼为可靠、一致且可解释的约束网络。整个神经符号交互过程无需先验领域知识即可从数据中恢复结构化符号模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种神经符号相互作用能有效将数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理结合，提供了一种自动化构建组合域模型的稳健方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法可有效减少约束获取中的人工交互，实现从数据自动构建可解释的符号约束模型，为组合领域的自动化建模提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [86] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出T-GADE，将热力学遗传算法扩展到LLM生成的"结构化产物"进化，通过基于LLM的遗传算子和产物级多样性评估，在在线装箱任务上降低了训练过剩并保持迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整合进化计算与LLM时需要同时控制种群多样性和生成能力。LLM输出的显式结构（如描述加代码）被称为结构化产物（artifact），已有方法（如EoH）缺乏对多样性的热力学调控机制。

Method: T-GADE扩展热力学遗传算法：采用LLM作为遗传算子，进行产物级多样性评估；以统一自由能目标支持世代式与稳态式更新；引入Fermi型占据（排除重复基因型）与Bose型占据（允许重复）。并给出精确的单成员移除规则及恢复EoH零温生存规则的条件。

Result: 在EoH的在线装箱任务上（过剩量衡量相对体积下界的箱数开销），世代式Bose型T-GADE在T=0.003时，20次运行中训练过剩中位数由1.152%降至0.815%，约降29%（双侧Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378）；两个最高排名最终候选的验证选择达到与EoH相同的迁移过剩中位数0.496%。

Conclusion: 结果表明热力学选择机制及对保留产物的验证式利用具有实用价值，能在保持迁移性能的同时改善训练表现。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [87] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多幕原型网络MEPN，通过聚合多个不相交支持幕的原型降低极低样本下原型方差，在DeFACTO工业传感器故障诊断中提升一shot性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断中标注故障样本稀少，标准原型网络在极低样本（very-low-shot）场景下因支持集过小导致类原型不稳定，决策可靠性下降。作者希望在不改变编码器结构的前提下缓解原型方差问题。

Method: MEPN聚合来自多个不相交支持幕（disjoint support episodes）的原型，以其均值作为最终类代表，从而降低原型方差，且不改动编码器。评估采用DeFACTO传感器数据集，进行五类故障分类，向真实工业测量注入合成偏差、漂移、尖峰和噪声故障，并与单幕基线及固定样本预算下的ProtoNet对比，共100次独立运行。

Result: 在单幕一shot设置（K=1，聚合N_agg=10个支持幕）下，MEPN显著优于单幕基线，达到论文所报性能指标；在相同10样本支持预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet统计上不可区分，说明提升来自原型累积机制而非固定预算下的学习优势。

Conclusion: 多幕原型聚合能有效稳定极低样本下的类原型，提升小样本工业故障诊断性能；其收益源于原型方差的降低，而非固定样本预算下的表征学习改进。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [88] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出简化运动学模型(KM)及三类混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)，将腕部IMU测量映射为全身质心(COM)加速度，在步态与坐立转换活动中验证了可行性并显著提升精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部IMU广泛用于日常健康监测，但无法充分反映全身动力学，而身体质心(COM)被视为生理参考标准。因此需要一种仅依赖腕部IMU即可估计COM加速度的方法，兼顾生物力学结构与数据驱动学习的优势。

Method: 提出基于若干简化假设、可单独由腕部IMU测量求解的简化运动学模型(KM)以实现腕部IMU到COM加速度的映射；并进一步提出三类混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)：串行学习(ser-)与两种同时学习方式(sim1-、sim2-)，将人类运动学模型与神经网络结合，融合灰箱与黑箱建模。使用包含10名健康志愿者、六种步态活动及坐立(SS)转换的腕部IMU与真实COM测量数据集进行训练与测试。

Result: KM模型误差在步态活动为6.7%–12.5%，SS为5.6%；HKM-NN模型显著提升性能，步态误差降至5.3%–9.3%，SS最佳误差为3.9%。在噪声测试条件下，sim1-/sim2-在高斯扰动下通常保持更强鲁棒性，而KM在椒盐噪声下鲁棒性相对较强。

Conclusion: 结果证明了由腕部IMU估计COM加速度的可行性，表明将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合，对在不完美、含噪测量条件下的可穿戴传感应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [89] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计Deep Perturbation Learning（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种角色，修正其实现错误后，DPL无法通过直接删除判据，其效用也劣于简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作用DPL较弱的角色（正则化项、对抗遗忘的暖启动）证据来支撑其最强主张（直接删除信号），缺乏在匹配协议下对每种角色的独立检验，因此需要严格验证。

Method: 在精确种子重训练基线的匹配协议下，分别测试DPL的三种角色；审计公开实现，发现两处正确性问题：图像方向在增强、归一化张量上计算却应用于原始图像，标签扰动低于float32精度导致标签未变。修正图像扰动流程后在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18上进行三对种子测试，并做单种子Tiny ImageNet检验。

Result: 修正后DPL在三对种子中均未通过直接删除判据；其效用效果符号在不同种子间不一致，且计入方向计算时间后不如简单暖启动基线。Tiny ImageNet单种子检验也不支持DPL作为正则化项或暖启动；发布代码中的预处理不一致使直接比较无法定论。

Conclusion: 结果仅适用于随机实例删除场景，不排除其他删除机制下的影响函数方法。作者发布了角色匹配的评估协议和基于扰动的删除主张审计清单。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [90] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一个面向多智能体、多用户LLM系统的隐私感知统一记忆框架，可持久管理私有与共享记忆，并发布首个多用户记忆评测数据集MUMBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM局限于单个用户会话，知识仅限单一对话，无法学习随时间演变的用户偏好；已有智能体记忆系统多停留在单用户层面，限制了可跨用户共享以改善下游响应的公共知识。

Method: AIM动态将信息分类为私有（仅限某用户、他人不可访问）或公共（所有用户可访问），并实施索引级访问控制，使私有记忆仅能被其所有者检索，从而在保护敏感数据的同时利用共享知识提升协调性与一致性。同时构建MUMBench多用户交互数据集，覆盖四个领域，包含私有与可共享信息，用于评测检索、创建、更新、删除等多种记忆操作。

Result: 在MUMBench上进行三次独立运行，AIM取得96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率以及70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM为多用户、多智能体LLM系统提供了隐私感知的持久记忆管理方案，MUMBench填补了多用户环境下多记忆操作评测数据集的空白，为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [91] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Affective Agent，一种可在穿戴级硬件上本地运行的个性化干预推理三层参考架构，结合亚十亿参数语言模型与生理、情境、用户历史证据，实现"是否、何时、如何干预"的决策，无需云端依赖或按用户重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算在可穿戴状态推断方面已有进展，但在设备端判断"是否、何时、如何"进行干预仍具挑战。现有方案多依赖云端或需按用户重训练，难以在穿戴级硬件上实现闭环个性化干预。

Method: 提出三层交互架构 Affective Agent（感知层、个性化层、推理层）；采用紧凑的亚十亿参数语言模型，融合生理证据、情境与用户历史；通过宿主管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，而非更新每用户权重；以两遍结构化推理在不确定性下做出干预决策。实例化于室内环境质量控制任务，并在留出的、由模拟器生成的纵向场景上进行评估，覆盖生理变化、情境、信号质量与干预历史等维度。

Result: 在这组合成评估中，记忆驱动的个性化与两遍结构化推理均提升了干预决策质量。

Conclusion: 通过将决策层迁移至设备端，本工作展示了从可穿戴状态推断走向穿戴级硬件上闭环、个性化干预的可行路径。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [92] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LoRA-RC：通过低秩修正在线自适应递归矩阵，并对核心矩阵施加谱范数球投影，保证收缩性与增量输入-状态稳定性；在Lorenz突变漂移任务上显著降低漂移后预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算（RC）仅训练线性读出，快速且数据高效，但固定储备池在系统漂移下退化；仅自适应读出不足，而无约束的储备池自适应又会破坏回声状态与增量稳定性等可靠性性质。

Method: 以流式预测误差驱动，对递归矩阵施加低秩修正；基础储备池与自适应基离线固定，仅在线更新小核心矩阵，并将其投影到谱范数球上、每步做低通滤波。投影确保每步所用递归矩阵落在可认证收缩集内，并沿在线自适应路径建立增量输入-状态稳定性界，其速率与增益与路径无关。

Result: 在发生参数突变漂移的Lorenz系统上，漂移后预测误差相比固定RC降低56%，相比仅自适应读出降低51%；20个随机种子的消融显示，去掉投影会使该误差放大约40倍以上。

Conclusion: LoRA-RC在保持RC稳定性理论保证的同时实现有约束的在线储备池自适应，有效应对系统漂移，且投影机制是性能与稳定性的关键。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CORE 方法，通过不确定性感知的信念修正，将逐轮局部证据与持久人设状态更新分离，选择性地更新有依据的用户偏好，并构建 PERSIST 基准测试人设状态在歧义、冲突和社交影响下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言模型面临"人设漂移"挑战：用户画像在长期交互中会演变而非永久固定。模型需要在偏好真正改变时修正持久人设状态，同时避免因短暂、模糊或未解决的观测而误更新。

Method: 提出 CORE 方法，将逐轮局部证据与持久人设状态的修正相分离，通过不确定性感知的信念修正机制，选择性地更新有依据支撑的用户偏好。同时构建 PERSIST 基准，作为锚点后的留出测试集，在顺序交互压力下评估人设状态鲁棒性，涵盖歧义、冲突和受控社交影响三类场景。

Result: 在 ALOE、PersonaChat 和 PERSIST 三个数据集上，CORE 提升了个性化对齐效果与鲁棒性，并在归一化封闭槽位状态保真度上取得互补性增益。人工评估与机制性控制实验进一步表明，其显式更新控制能力超越了单纯更强的生成能力或持久记忆机制。

Conclusion: 显式的、不确定性感知的人设更新控制对于个性化语言模型的长期鲁棒性至关重要，仅依靠更强的生成或持久记忆并不足够。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [94] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BlueLM-GUI，一个35B-A3B移动GUI智能体，以真机为中心的飞轮（Every Sample Matters / Every Rollout Is Real / Every Query Evolves）三大原则，在MobileGUI-VBench和AndroidWorld上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体正从多模块框架转向端到端原生模型，但工业部署存在三个持续缺口：沙盒训练与生产环境分布不匹配；昂贵的真机失败未被充分利用；固定基准饱和，失去指导迭代的能力。

Method: 三大原则：1) Every Sample Matters——双轨流水线，含异构三系统共识评估与错误纠正与派生模块，将每条轨迹转化为可用监督；2) Every Rollout Is Real——三阶段配方（持续预训练、监督微调、在数百台真机上做智能体强化学习），使能力直接迁移到部署；3) Every Query Evolves——配额驱动的基准方法，三个正交维度支持精准归因并随模型升级而系统演进。

Result: MobileGUI-VBench达87.4，超最佳闭源模型5.1分；AndroidWorld达84.9，开源模型最优，与闭源模型相当。

Conclusion: 将模型训练与迭代改进同时根植于真机和三大Every原则，可产生强大、鲁棒且可迁移的移动GUI能力。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [95] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）中神经网络参数化的趋势，沿五个维度对19种代表性方法进行分类，并通过受控实验证明"选择性神经化"优于全面神经化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS结合了显式图元与高效光栅化，但近期系统越来越多地使用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数。这一趋势需要一个系统性的刻画框架，因为不同的神经化选择解决的是不同的局限性，不能简单地用"是否使用神经网络"这一二元标签来概括。

Method: 沿五个维度对神经参数化进行分类：属性解码（attribute decoding）、空间共享（spatial sharing）、视角条件解码（view-conditioned decoding）、拓扑生成（topology generation）和摊销推理（amortized inference）。基于此框架分析了19种代表性方法。此外，在受控的mip-NeRF 360实验中分离出三种神经参数化形式进行对比验证。

Result: 对19种方法的分析表明，不同的神经化选择针对不同的局限性，无法简化为二元标签。受控实验显示：共享外观和透明度参数能够提升重建质量，而解码几何结构则没有带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 证据支持"选择性神经化"策略：当共享函数能够捕获可复用的相关性、同时不牺牲显式splat的局部几何自由度时，能够带来帮助。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [96] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了The Core这一无传统适应度函数的竞争性协同进化算法，通过将智能体生态位划分到原始环境的特定子集，验证其在子环境中的表现优于生态位为整个系统或其他子环境的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Core此前已被证明能在Xpilot游戏中进化出具备战斗与导航能力的智能体，但尚不清楚将智能体生态位限制到原始环境的特定子集是否能带来更好的控制效果，因此本文探索生态位划分策略对进化成功率的影响。

Method: 沿用The Core的竞争性协同进化框架，通过局部交互、锦标赛选择、交叉和变异产生后代，无需传统适应度函数；在此基础上将智能体进行生态位划分（niching），分别针对原始环境的不同子环境进行进化，并与面向整个系统及其他子环境的智能体进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，面向特定子环境进化的生态位智能体在相应子环境中取得成功的能力，优于面向整个系统进化的智能体，也优于面向其他不同子环境进化的智能体。

Conclusion: 将智能体生态位划分到特定子环境能够提升The Core算法进化出的控制器在对应任务中的表现，说明环境细化与生态位划分是增强该无适应度协同进化算法效果的有效手段。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [97] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA 提出一种线性注意力解码框架，利用生成式推荐中共享提示与短分歧后缀的特点，用单一共享状态加紧凑追加记录表示所有 beam，在不重建完整循环状态的情况下完成解码，实现1.54-2.46倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖大 beam 解码生成数百候选物品，给循环线性注意力带来新的扩展挑战。现有线性注意力服务系统要么为每个 beam 物化完整循环状态，要么反复重放共享历史，导致显著的内存与流量开销。

Method: OneLA 用单一由提示派生的共享状态加紧凑的、仅追加的分歧转移记录来表示所有 beam 状态；据此仅计算每个解码步所需的状态信息，而不为每个 beam 重建完整循环状态。同时使用轻量级祖先索引追踪构成各 beam 历史的转移记录，使 beam 更新无需移动或复制已有记录，并通过融合 GPU 内核跨 beam 复用共享状态。

Result: 分析表明 OneLA 实现1.54-2.46倍端到端解码加速，同时大幅降低循环状态内存占用与数据移动。

Conclusion: OneLA 通过共享状态与追加式分歧记录的高效表示及融合内核，有效缓解了生成式推荐大 beam 解码下的内存与带宽瓶颈。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [98] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化进化算法，独立进化六足机器人每条腿的步态，在Webots仿真环境下优化单腿控制器，无需中心协调即可涌现协调运动，性能优于协同协同进化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统六足机器人步态优化通常依赖集中式协调，随着腿数增加，优化空间维度高、复杂度大，难以实现可扩展与自适应的运动控制，因此作者希望用去中心化策略降低优化难度。

Method: 在Webots仿真器中使用Mantis六足机器人平台，采用去中心化进化算法分别独立进化每条腿的控制器，不设置中心协调机制；并将该方法与协同协同进化（cooperative coevolution）方法进行对比基准测试。

Result: 去中心化方法在生成稳定且自适应步态方面表现更优，同时展现出有趣的涌现式协调行为，验证了独立进化腿控制器的有效性。

Conclusion: 独立进化腿控制器可降低步态优化复杂度，去中心化策略在可扩展和自适应机器人系统方面具有潜力，为多足机器人运动控制提供新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出过程感知语言认知诊断框架（PLCD），利用大语言模型构建概念图式与认知过程图，并通过目标条件语义记忆和过程级对比学习将文本证据映射到统一认知空间，提升预测性能与认知迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型依赖离散的、基于ID的嵌入来表示学生、习题和概念，这与学习者认知中知识并非以孤立符号存储和检索的本质相悖，导致在新习题或新概念出现时存在语义局限，即冷启动鲁棒性差。

Method: PLCD框架以语言衍生的结构作为认知先验，并用作答记录校准学生后验状态。具体包括：利用大语言模型构建概念图式和认知过程图；使用目标条件语义记忆检索与每个目标习题相关的历史作答；通过带DA-MoE专家的过程奠基式Language-to-Cognition Mapper，结合过程级对比学习，将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。

Result: 实验结果表明，PLCD在预测学生表现方面优于传统基线，并展现出较强的认知迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将大语言模型的计算能力与测量潜在知识状态的心理测量目标相连接，表明由作答记录校准的结构化语言先验能够提升冷启动鲁棒性和认知基础性。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [100] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 VRL-Bench 用于在有限试错预算下公平评估语言智能体的试错学习，发现现有语言记忆方法（如 Reflexion 等）效果不稳定，并提出 VEX² 调度器在全部六种设置下取得正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试错学习（如 Reflexion 的语言强化）被认为能提升语言智能体在计算机控制等复杂任务上的表现，但缺乏在有限试验预算下的公平评估框架，且各方法的实际增益可能不稳定。

Method: 提出 VRL-Bench 评估框架，在 MiniWoB 与 WebShop 上对三个模型、多种语言记忆更新方法进行对比；通过重放实验分析反思带来的探索与利用权衡；进一步提出 VEX²，一种语言模型驱动的探索-利用调度器，联合选择策略并分配剩余试验预算。

Result: 各语言记忆方法在部分设置下优于无记忆重试，但在其他设置下反而降低成功率；重放实验显示反思可能降低成功率，体现利用经验与持续探索之间的权衡；VEX² 是唯一在全部六种设置下均取得正向成功率增益的方法。

Conclusion: 现有语言记忆式试错学习方法的效果并不稳健，需要显式平衡探索与利用；VEX² 通过调度策略选择和预算分配，在有限试验预算下实现了更一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [101] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇SoK（知识系统化）论文，围绕完整智能体执行流程重构越狱安全研究，提出统一攻击/防御分类法与"安全—效用—效率"评估框架，并通过受控实证研究揭示了现代LLM与智能体AI时代既有越狱安全结论的三个关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从对话助手快速演变为具备推理、规划、调用工具、持久记忆、智能体间通信与多步任务执行能力的智能体系统；同时现代模型的固有安全对齐显著强于早期模型，而大量越狱攻击与防御最初正是在早期模型上研究的。这一转变引出一个根本问题：在智能体AI时代，哪些既有的越狱安全发现仍然成立。论文即针对这一知识有效性问题展开。

Method: 采用Systematization of Knowledge（SoK）方法，围绕完整智能体执行流程重构越狱安全：构建覆盖用户交互、规划与推理、记忆、工具使用、智能体间通信的统一攻击与防御分类法；提出区分固有有害提示安全性、对抗性越狱鲁棒性与智能体级安全结果的"安全—效用—效率"评估框架；并在统一智能体框架内对代表性攻击与防御进行受控实证研究。

Result: 实证研究揭示三个重要缺口：一是强固有对齐并不意味着对对抗性越狱的鲁棒性；二是防御有效性高度依赖模型、攻击类型与组件，并可能带来过度拒答、效用下降与延迟方面的显著代价；三是最终响应层面的低攻击成功率可能掩盖严重的中间环节失陷——即使最终响应被成功过滤，规划、记忆与工具交互仍可能不安全。

Conclusion: 研究结果推动越狱防御从以响应为中心转向跨层、执行感知的安全范式，在保护智能体状态、组件间转换与外部动作的同时，兼顾实际效用与效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HORIZON分层智能体，用于Lux AI Season 3部分可观测与随机化环境下的对战任务，融合对称感知、双记忆信念跟踪、遗物图注意力、信息增益探索与对手条件策略混合，在胜率与联赛评分上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3要求智能体在部分可观测、关卡动态随机化以及五局三胜的赛制下作战，既要战术执行又需快速适应，现有方法难以兼顾短程控制与跨局元推理。

Method: HORIZON为分层智能体，包含对称感知空间建模、双记忆信念跟踪、以遗物为中心的图注意力、信息增益驱动的探索以及对手条件策略混合；将短时程控制与跨局元推理分离，并引入辅助信念与世界模型目标以稳定学习，在大规模JAX模拟器中用PPO训练。

Result: 实验显示，在比赛胜率、单局胜率、适应增益和联赛评分上，HORIZON相较强循环网络与前馈基线均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 该智能体能够显式推断隐藏游戏参数与对手风格，验证了分层设计、信念建模与元推理结合在复杂部分可观测对抗环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style. Experiments show consistent gains in match win rate, episode win rate, adaptation gain, and league rating over strong recurrent and feed forward baselines.

</details>


### [103] [LifeFuse-Mem: Lifecycle-Aware State Fusion Against Temporary Overwriting for Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12436)
*Hanyu Zhao,Yuqian Feng,Zhenyu Song,Yuanchao Cheng,Yance Jiao,Tengfei Pan,Li Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LifeFuse-Mem，一种生命周期感知的神经记忆框架，通过区分信息的时效承诺来缓解长期LLM智能体中的记忆覆写问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的LLM智能体需要能在多次交互间维持连贯内部状态的记忆机制。现有机制难以区分应长期影响未来交互的信息与仅影响当前上下文的临时信息，二者生命周期错配会导致临时信息覆写持久知识，引发持久智能体的行为漂移。

Method: 作者研究了"生命周期标注"的记忆设定：训练时写入片段提供生命周期元数据，评估时采用相位感知读出。在此设定下，提出LifeFuse-Mem框架，使用专用记忆组件与生命周期感知更新，使稳定知识与瞬态知识在本地独立演化，避免将临时上下文转为持久状态。

Result: 在受控的反覆写基准上，LifeFuse-Mem提升了采集受控的保持能力并减少了临时覆写；在两个公开长期记忆基准上，保持总体竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式的生命周期信号有助于诊断并缓解紧凑在线记忆中的覆写问题。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents require memory mechanisms that maintain coherent internal states across interactions. We study a lifecycle-labeled memory setting in which write episodes provide lifecycle metadata during training, and phase-aware readout is used during evaluation. This setting reflects the need to distinguish information that should remain influential across future interactions from information that should affect only the current context.
